[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 3.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] Player 2 was killed during the night.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.
[Player 5]  means the Mafia didn’t target Player 3, so maybe they’re trying to eliminate someone else.

Player 6: I swear I didn’t kill anyone. I was just trying to stay out of the way.

Player 7: I think we should vote now. Let’s not waste time.

**Round 2 Discussion Complete.**

 Round 3 Discussion:

Player 0: I still think Player 6 is the Mafia. They’re too quiet, and they haven’t given us any real information.

Player 1: I agree with Player 0. Player 6 is acting suspiciously.

Player 3: I think we should also consider Player 1. They’re the one who started the suspicion against Player 6, and that might be a sign they’re trying to throw us off.

Player 4: I think we need to be careful. We don’t want to vote out an innocent person.

Player 6: I swear I’m not the Mafia. I just want to survive.

Player 7: I think we should vote now. Let’s not waste any more time.

**Round 3 Discussion Complete.**

 Voting begins. Cast your vote for the player you believe is the Mafia.

Please vote now.
[Player 1]  Player 1, it is your turn to vote. Please choose a player to eliminate. Valid choices: [0], [3], [4], [6], [7].

[COMPLETION]
